# Algebra simplification test cases

> ## Result: **2173 / 2500 passed (86.9%)**
> Run on 2026-10-23 against `master` @ c3af7e3 with `LatexCalculator::evaluate` (default settings) and `simplify` for the restrictions.
>
> | Outcome | Cases |
> |---|---:|
> | PASS | 2173 |
> | FAIL, only extra restrictions on factors without real roots (e.g. `x^2 + x + 1 != 0`) | 326 |
> | FAIL, other | 0 |
> | ERROR | 1 |
>
> Lenient pass rate, counting the extra-restriction cases as correct since they exclude no real number: **2499 / 2500 (99.96%)**.
>
> | Difficulty | Cases | PASS | FAIL (extra restr.) | FAIL (other) | ERROR |
> |---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
> | 1-5 Easy | 125 | 121 | 4 | 0 | 0 |
> | 6-20 Medium | 375 | 322 | 53 | 0 | 0 |
> | 21-50 Hard | 750 | 651 | 99 | 0 | 0 |
> | 51-100 Very hard | 1250 | 1079 | 170 | 0 | 1 |
>
> **Pass criteria**: the actual output must be mathematically equal to the expected output (checked with exact rational arithmetic at sample points) *and* the restrictions must match the expected list exactly. Different but equal spellings still pass and are noted as `equal but written differently`. Multivariate products are written by Quantus as `2 \cdot a \cdot b` where this file writes `2a b`; those count as equal.

2500 test cases for the algebraic simplification pipeline (`LatexCalculator::evaluate` with free variables), from polynomial order 1 to 100, 25 cases per order. The last four columns hold the recorded results.

## Conventions

- **Order**: the highest polynomial degree that appears in the input (for multivariate cases: the highest total degree).
- **Difficulty**: order 1–5 Easy, 6–20 Medium, 21–50 Hard, 51–100 Very hard.
- **Input**: LaTeX exactly as it is typed into Quantus.
- **Expected output**: the fully simplified form, written like `serializer::algebraic_expression` (expanded polynomials, a single reduced fraction otherwise). Any mathematically equal form that is fully reduced counts as correct (e.g. factored instead of expanded); note the difference in *Notes*.
- **Domain restrictions**: written like `format_restrictions_plain`, e.g. `x != -2, -1, 1, x^2 + 4 != 0`. Real excluded values are listed as numbers. A factor with no real zero (such as `x^2 + 4`) is listed as `p != 0` when it appears as a binomial factor of a denominator; it excludes no real number. `none` means there is no restriction. Restrictions of the *input* are kept even when the factor cancels.
- **Variables**: `x`, plus `a`, `b`, `z` in multivariate cases. Letters that are units (`m`, `s`, `g`, `y`, `t`, …) are not used.
- **Pass/Fail**: `PASS`, `FAIL` or `ERROR` (engine error / no result).

Every input was checked against its expected output with exact rational arithmetic at several random points, and every restriction against the values that make a denominator zero, when this file was generated. They were run through Quantus on 2026-10-23; the results are in the last four columns.
